# Model Scoreboard

This file records accepted and rejected model candidates for live FourMeme trading. Selection is based on live-sized replay with 10% position sizing, gas costs, current execution delay assumptions, walk-forward checks, and stress replay.

## 2026-09-22 Scanner Selectivity Corrected Against Chain Ground Truth

- **What was wrong:** the candidate sweep watched tokens by creation time (create-event watch list plus a 60 minute age window). Ground truth from raw Four.meme logs for a 30 minute window: 15 tokens traded, exactly one met the bar (`0x612b22e8493e…`, 7 buyers, 42.27 buy volume), and every one of them had been created hours earlier. The age window excluded all of them and the channel was pushing 0.05 dust instead. The biggest mover of the previous hour (352 in buy volume) was sitting in the collector's memory the whole time, invisible to a watch list fed only by creation events.
- **Fix:** the scanner now follows a rolling activity window built from purchase/sale events (all ABI versions), replayed from the chain at startup, with the bar evaluated on the window (fresh buyers, minimum buy volume, net inflow) and age reported but never filtered. A second defect found while making it live: the chain seed sent bare topic hashes to `eth_getLogs`, which the node rejects, so the first seed silently returned nothing.
- **Verification:** after the rewrite the same ground-truth query returned that one token, and the scanner pushed exactly it. Alerts are now persisted as `candidate_alert` rows, which also carries the hourly dedupe across restarts. Two further defects surfaced while watching it run: the chain seed sent bare topic hashes to `eth_getLogs` (node rejects them, so restarts seeded nothing), and the sweep's log line collided with its own `top` key so every sweep raised *after* sending its alert — two delivered alerts were invisible and `qualifying=0` looked like a filter bug when it was dedupe. Counters are now `over_bar / suppressed / pushed` with a regression test for the loop.
- **Decision:** no model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing or trading switch changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. Candidate alerts remain labelled as scan results, not trade authorisations. Scoreboard updated because the scanner's selection rule changed materially and its selectivity is now measured against the chain rather than assumed.

## 2026-09-21 Live Candidate Scanning

- **Operating state:** the collector now scans continuously instead of only reacting to graduations. Every 60s it re-checks watched tokens and pushes the ones with real buyer flow; 5 candidate alerts were delivered to Telegram during the run, and the scanner database holds 1314 launches, 635 snapshots, 511 safety reports and 511 decisions from the same window.
- **Bar chosen from measurement:** over 459 tokens in one hour on 2026-09-21, `>=1` fresh buyer (bought and not sold) plus net inflow matched 36 tokens, `>=2` matched 4, `>=3` matched 0. The default is therefore 2, with the measurement recorded in `.env.example`. This is an operating choice for a readable feed, not a validated edge.
- **Defects found while making it live:** the watch set keyed tokens lowercase while the collector stores the checksummed address (every lookup missed, nothing pushed); a restart started with an empty watch set and ignored the preceding hour; seeding only the newest file missed tokens held by earlier runs; and after a restart the in-memory record is thinner than the flushed one, so the sweep now takes whichever shows more distinct buyers. A separate defect disabled the whole Telegram channel: `candidate` was missing from the env action whitelist, and a test now asserts every action the formatter can emit is accepted by the contract.
- **Decision:** no model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing or trading switch changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. Candidate alerts are explicitly labelled as scan results, not trade authorisations, and buy signals keep the fail-closed safe-mode requirement. Scoreboard updated because the scanner's live operating mode and its bar are risk-relevant facts.

## 2026-09-21 Collector Restart: Backfill Gap Closed

- **What happened:** restarting the collector to load the audited code exposed a silent data-loss path. `resume_max_catchup_blocks` was hard-coded to 256, so a checkpoint 38037 blocks behind was truncated to the chain head and that whole range was skipped from collection — one warning line, no other signal.
- **Closure:** the range was re-collected with `scripts/backfill_fourmeme_month_raw.py --from-block 123160296 --to-block 123198076` against the endpoint the collector itself uses. 38 chunks, 4516 tokens, output in `data/training/gap_20260921/` with `backfill_summary.json`. The first attempt against the script's default endpoint (`bsc.rpc.blxrbdn.com`) only produced repeated read timeouts and range bisection, so the working endpoint matters.
- **Code change:** `COLLECTOR_RESUME_MAX_CATCHUP_BLOCKS` now controls the window and `0` disables truncation, so a restart can keep backfilling from the checkpoint instead of dropping the range. Default stays 256. Covered by four tests.
- **Decision:** no model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing or trading switch changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. Scoreboard updated because the collection window and a recovered data gap are operational facts about the training corpus.

## 2026-09-21 Telegram Channel Live Run

- **Live verification:** with the operator's own bot and chat, `getMe`/`getChat` succeeded and five real messages were delivered across four paths: `signal-test`, a full-chain `buy` on a synthetic safe snapshot, a rate-limit/dedupe probe (2 delivered, 2 dropped as designed), and the collector path (`LiquidityAdded` → schedule → `asyncio.to_thread` → scan → push). A real `signal-scan` on a live Four.meme token queried GoPlus (200), DexScreener (200), honeypot.is (404 for that token) and GMGN (`gmgn_api_key_missing`) and correctly rejected.
- **Defects found by the live run:** the CLI never called `load_dotenv()`, so `signal-test`/`signal-scan` reported missing credentials while a valid `.env` sat in the repo root; and `--config` ignored `SCANNER_CONFIG`, so the CLI silently ran in the default `learning` mode that cannot authorise a buy. Both fixed and covered by tests.
- **Runtime state:** the collector was restarted on the audited code with `SCANNER_ENABLED=true`, `SCANNER_CONFIG=config/scanner_signal.json` (mode `safe`) and `SCANNER_SIGNAL_EVENTS=graduation`. Automatic scans were proven live by temporarily scanning `launch` as well (18 scans in 75s, in-flight cap enforced, all `reject`), then reverted. Telegram credentials live only in the gitignored `.env`; a leak check found the token in no tracked file and in neither log.
- **Decision:** no model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing or trading switch changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. Signal supply is now limited by provider coverage (missing GMGN key, honeypot.is 404s, and market caps that arrive only once DexScreener indexes a token), not by the delivery layer. Scoreboard updated intentionally because live delivery and the collector's signal wiring are now operational facts.

## 2026-09-21 Telegram Channel Verification Round

- **Verification:** full suite run with both the system interpreter and `./venv/bin/python` (1599 tests, 1 skipped, 0 failures each), `compileall` clean, pyflakes clean apart from three intentional re-exports, and every module under `src/`, `config/`, `tools/` and `scripts/` imported in the venv (239 modules, 0 failures). Static scans: 0 mutable default arguments, 0 blocking `time.sleep` inside an async function, 0 `requests` calls without a timeout.
- **Defects found and fixed in this pass:** (1) the channel could never fire — only the e2e smoke script and the tests called `decide()`, so an enabled channel would have stayed silent forever; `ScannerPipeline.scan()` now runs snapshot → safety → decision → signal in one call and the collector can schedule it from real events behind `SCANNER_SIGNAL_EVENTS` (empty by default). (2) `ScannerConfig.mode` defaults to `learning`, and the decision layer refuses to buy on a learning report, so automatic scans are now skipped with an explicit warning in that mode and `signal-scan` forces `--safety-mode safe`. (3) truncating an over-long HTML body could cut a tag and make Telegram reject the whole message, so long bodies are downgraded to plain text. (4) the env contract test reloaded `config.notify_config`, which rebinds the module to a new class while `src/notify/telegram.py` keeps the old one — the same order-dependency bug fixed earlier in the trading tests.
- **Environment-independent suite:** two default-contract tests failed on any machine whose local `.env` overrides `BUY_ACTION_POLICY_ROUTER_SHADOW_AUDIT_ENABLED` or `BUY_ACTION_POLICY_CONTINUE_HOLD_ACTIVATION_PCT` (both assertions are unchanged since `43d5a9f`, so this was a false alarm, not a regression). The router-default test now skips with an explicit message when the value is overridden, and the position-metadata test asserts the configured `TradingConfig` value instead of a literal. The suite is now green with and without `PYTHON_DOTENV_DISABLED=1`.
- **Decision:** no model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing, `.env` trading switch or live-risk interpretation changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. The channel remains off by default, read-only, and unable to reach the executor or a private key. Scoreboard updated intentionally because the signal path's reachability and the mode requirement are risk-relevant operating facts.

## 2026-09-21 Full-Repository Code Audit — Fail-Open And Chain-Routing Fixes

- **Audit:** Static and manual review of every module under `src/`, `config/`, `tools/` and `scripts/` (≈79k lines) plus the live data directories. Evidence: `docs/research/20260921-code-audit/README.md` and `evidence.json`. Full suite moved from 1538 tests (baseline, 1 skipped, 0 failures) to 1556 tests, 1 skipped, 0 failures; `compileall` clean; pyflakes shows only three intentional re-exports.
- **Risk-relevant fixes:** `src/decision/engine.py` could turn a `learning`-mode safety report into `action=buy` (the learning profile only blocks on `honeypot_sim`), now rejected with `safety_mode_not_safe`; `src/core/trader.py` rounded sell amounts down to 1e9-wei multiples and silently skipped sub-step 6-decimal balances, now refuses loudly; `chainId: 56` was hard-coded in five signed transactions and is now `MEME_CHAIN_ID`/`TradingConfig.CHAIN_ID`; GoPlus/DexScreener were pinned to BSC endpoints while accepting a chain argument, and `ScannerPipeline` defaulted every chain to chain id 56, so a non-BSC token could be scored with BSC data — chain ids now come from `config/chains.json` and unknown chains fail closed; `src/trader/bot.py` now writes position state atomically and refuses to start when the state file cannot be parsed.
- **Accepted risks recorded:** the PancakeSwap `minOut=1` emergency exit stays (it only fires when the pool will not quote, and refusing to sell can trap funds), and `src/walletflow/gmgn.py` cannot yet distinguish a provider failure from "no smart-money trades".
- **Depth limit:** `src/pipeline/train_hybrid.py`, `src/data/collector.py`, `src/data/dataset_builder.py`, the `*_probe.py` research scripts, `src/rl/` and `src/model/` were scanned structurally, not read line by line.
- **Data cleanup:** 64 paths / 726 MiB removed after checking that `docs/`, `src/`, `scripts/`, `tests/` and `config/` do not reference them — stale `logs/bot.log` (196 MB, last write 2026-06-10), May training/replay logs, caches, 24 experiment model directories from the withdrawn 2026-09-10/11/12 rounds, derived `data/datasets/*`, and two unreferenced derived windows. Kept: the live `data/training/` output, every window referenced by a research document, `bsc_month_raw_20260911_run7`, the model directory `.env` loads, `data/research/`, `.env`, and both live logs.
- **Decision:** No model trained or promoted, no threshold, sizing, `.env` trading switch or live-risk interpretation changed, and no on-chain transaction sent. The running collector was not restarted, so it still executes pre-audit code until its next start. Scoreboard updated intentionally because this round changed the live-risk reading of the decision layer, the sell path and the chain-routing boundary.

## 2026-09-20 Dog-Bot Architecture Research

- **Research:** Read eight open-source sniping/scanner implementations plus official Chainstack, Helius, Jito, BNB Chain (MEV/PBS, EVN, hard forks), bloXroute (BSC private transactions, Solana `submit-snipe`), NodeReal, GoPlus and PumpPortal documentation. Two references are production-grade (chainstacklabs/pumpfun-bonkfun-bot, 0xfnzero/sol-trade-sdk); one has a strong filter/paper-trading design (Memcoin-Sniper-Bot); several popular "Four.meme sniper" repos contain no new-token listener, empty rug-detection stubs, or dangerous `minOut=0` and key-handling patterns. Report: `docs/research/20260920-dogbot-architecture/summary.md`. A follow-up pass on live products (DefiLlama protocol fees on 2026-09-20: Axiom ≈$2.29M/24h, GMGN ≈$0.99M/24h, Terminal ≈$0.17M/24h, Maestro/Trojan/Photon lower; Axiom Tweet Monitor, GMGN X Tracker + SnipeX, Maestro Telegram call-channel Signals, Cielo wallet-Mindshare, Axiom/Trench bundle checkers) is recorded in `docs/research/20260920-dogbot-architecture/live-products.md`. Production terminals use launch/migration events as the trigger and tracked-wallet flow as the confirmation; social heat is an alert/ranking layer, not the sole trigger.
- **Direction change:** Next implementation priority is measurement and protection: a cached pre-trade risk gate (honeypot simulation, holder concentration, dev history, bundled/sniper counts), shadow execution with realistic costs and per-filter P&L attribution, exit ladders plus LP-drop watcher and daily/weekly circuit breakers, and private transaction paths for execution. On-chain events remain the trigger; the attention board stays candidate discovery only. No verified aggregate profitability data was found, and search-provider outages are disclosed in the report.
- **Design:** `docs/plans/2026-09-20-scanner-system-design.md` proposes six layers — on-chain event trigger, hard safety filters with fail-closed semantics, wallet-flow confirmation (deployer quality, KOL scout score with n≥30, first-touch + followers, net inflow), three-column heat ranking (market/social/funding), shadow execution with per-filter P&L attribution, and only then limited live trading. Individual-operator evidence (meme-radar read-only radar thresholds, MadeOnSol KOL first-touch backtest, Hicarus dev "Distributor" pattern, crypdev007 shreds+gRPC claim, X-monitor/TG-MemeGPT social auto-trade) is in `docs/research/20260920-dogbot-architecture/individual-operators.md`. The detailed P0–P9 implementation plan is `docs/plans/2026-09-20-scanner-system-plan.md` (written, not started). No runtime change in this round.
- **Decision:** Research only. No model trained or promoted, no threshold/sizing change, no `.env` trading switch, no bot/collector process change, no live-risk reinterpretation. Scoreboard updated intentionally because the next direction changed.

## 2026-09-16 Profitability Evidence Audit — Earlier Heat Claims Withdrawn

- **Denomination/selection audit:** Current manager metadata was retrieved through the configured proxy for all 615 frozen heat candidates: 18 native BNB, 381 USDT, 169 GMEB and 47 other nonnative quotes. All 22 former near-target “executable” observations are nonnative. Current executor rejects nonnative quote assets; 16/18 native candidates already migrated before the assumed curve entry. These are later audit metadata, not historical signals; attempts at historical state on four providers did not yield valid old state. Complete evidence and 615-row CSV: `docs/research/20260916-bsc-profitability-evidence/candidate-audit.json` / `.csv`.
- **Decoder root cause confirmed:** ABI signature and an actual RPC log prove `c18aa711…` is `LiquidityAdded`, not TokenSale. The repeated fake seller is the numeric offers amount interpreted as an address. Filtering prices below `1e-15` was not a valid repair. New `src/data/fourmeme_log_decoder.py` fixes typed decoding for the offline raw backfill, including legacy quantities/fees. Existing snapshots have not been rebuilt, and the running listener/collector was not changed.
- **Execution claim correction:** A different user's trade within three seconds is neither necessary nor sufficient for our AMM execution. Reports 70/71/73 do not prove 22 trades, 19 wins or three true losses. Old market-cap/USD figures are also invalid without quote-asset normalization and historical FX. All 615 actual fills/P&L remain unknown. The old raw outputs are retained; `evidence-status.json` registers their withdrawn interpretation. Tail/cross-boundary replay also has time, quote, boundary, and capital-accounting issues and cannot select a profitability winner pending repair.
- **Recommendation, not accepted alpha:** Test an attributable narrative event, contract mapping and independent net funding response before limited-capital entry; exit weak positions and retain only persistent trends. Free-first prototype: curated X notifications/manual original-post input plus local chain verification, with an on-chain-only control. Compare a few frozen-entry conditional exits rather than resweeping an invalid USD threshold. `recommendation.md` defines the practical protocol and its limits. No fresh ROI, trained model or live candidate is claimed.
- **Closeout:** Scoreboard updated intentionally because both prior risk interpretation and next strategy direction changed. Local research/code only; no .env, model/sizing/trading switch, bot/collector process, docs/goals, commit or push change.

## 2026-09-14 X / Heat-Source Redesign

- **Research direction:** X is a plausible discovery clock, not a trade label. The official X docs fetched for this round describe recent search (seven days), paid/Enterprise full-archive search, post timestamps/author/metrics, and filtered-stream delivery around 6-7 seconds P99. Powerstream is lower latency but premium. Source files are `docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/46-fetch-x_filtered_stream-20260914.md` and `47-fetch-x_powerstream-20260914.md`.
- **Current limitation:** There is no local historical X capture with receive timestamps, so an X signal cannot yet be replayed as a live edge. DEX Screener/GeckoTerminal smoke tests also confirm that current snapshots are mixed-chain or as-of views rather than historical event streams (`docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/38-heat-source-api-smoke-20260914.json`, `39-heat-source-field-sample-20260914.json`).
- **Decision:** Add X only as a shadow discovery source. Require address mapping, chain confirmation, independent on-chain buyer/flow/liquidity evidence, realistic receive-to-entry delay, and a matched on-chain-only control before any buy. AI may extract/classify the post but cannot authorize a trade. No `.env`, model path, sizing, trading enablement, bot behavior, or collector runtime was changed.
- **Scoreboard status:** This round intentionally adds no model candidate or live result; the scoreboard note records the changed next direction before task closeout.

## 2026-09-14 Free Market-Cap / X Heat Gate

- **Audit correction:** The original `50-market-cap-heat-gate-profile-20260914.json` used the first event after each horizon without an upper bound and included 58 synthetic graduation-system prices. Its aggregate result is invalid evidence and must not be used for model selection.
- **Corrected per-candidate replay:** `70-market-cap-heat-failure-attribution-20260914.json` keeps a hard horizon boundary, filters synthetic prices, and separates snapshot from executable exits. Of 615 `$50k` activity+flow candidates, only 22 had a 1-hour event within the 3-second exit grace; 3 were negative and 19 positive. The remaining 593 lack timely executable exit evidence. The three true failures are detailed in `71-market-cap-true-executable-failures-20260914.json`.
- **Decision:** Do not hard-code `$30k-$100k` or buy every pass. Before any threshold comparison, fix quote/exit coverage, add an early adverse-move veto and heat-decay/sell-wave exit, and join valid post-graduation DEX data. A KOL/X post still requires exact contract mapping and chain response.
- **Comparable products:** GMGN provides real-time smart-money/KOL/first-buyer views and BSC Agent API capabilities but requires credentials and warns labels are not guaranteed realtime. LunarCrush's free Hobby tier is market-data-only; social/creator endpoints are paid. Santiment exposes social-volume/trending metrics but API access and historical/realtime restrictions are plan-gated. Kaito provides the mindshare/attention-economy benchmark, not a verified free replay feed. Evidence is preserved in `docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/51-*` through `69-*`.
- **Scoreboard status:** No model candidate or live result was added. `docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/68-x-heat-leaderboard-research-20260914.md` defines the free-first leaderboard and source-specific gates; `.env`, model paths, sizing, trading enablement, bot behavior, and collector runtime remain unchanged.

It uses complete paths for training and includes incomplete paths in capital replay. ## 2026-09-14 Staged DEX Confirmation

- **Rejected / research only:** The staged path waits for a causal post-graduation DEX health check before entering. A 24-rule grid selected `3` bars, an `8h` wait, prior-day market-volume ratio `0.5`, price retention `-50%`, and volume retention `0.05`; validation was `-0.2433 BNB` and final was `-0.3304 BNB`. Report: `docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/32-dex-confirmation-grid-20260914.json`.
- **Fresh ranker:** The model trained on the selected confirmation path produced `2` final 6h trades for `-0.0008 BNB`, no 24h final trades, and `-0.3468 BNB` at 72h. No complete-path, stress, or walk-forward acceptance gate passed. Report: `docs/research/20260913-bsc-strategy-foundation/33-dex-confirmation-selected-20260914.json`.
- **Decision:** Keep this code as a research protocol, but do not promote it to runtime. The current BSC dataset does not demonstrate a repeatable edge even after delayed DEX confirmation; external historical attention/liquidity data and a new independent window are required.

## 2026-09-13 Entry Barrier / Long-Horizon Research

- **Rejected / research only:** `scripts/profile_target_barriers.py` evaluated 3,022 entry candidates from the latest month without using graduation. At `+20%/-30%` barriers, conservative equal-stake total return was `-25,065.9%` over 1h, `-29,252.9%` over 6h, `-30,682.2%` over 24h, and `-31,313.8%` over 72h. The gate grid (buyers, buy count, and entry age) selected `4 buyers / 8 buys / 300s` on validation, but its final total remained negative.
- **Censor stress:** Re-running the same grid with every path ending before the horizon scored as a full loss made the validation-selected `+20%/-50%` configuration the least negative, but final totals were still about `-30,199%` (1h), `-37,909%` (6h), and `-47,165%` (24h). This is a data-coverage warning, not evidence to loosen the stop.
- **Rejected / research only:** Fresh `barrier_classifier` and `profit_ranker` experiments in `data/models/experimental_entry_ranker_*` used decision-time features and chronological splits. The 6h ranker looked positive only under the censor-prone `last_observation` policy (`+144.8%` total return for final top-50); complete-path total was `-100.3%`, and the strict `loss` censor policy was negative. No model, threshold, gate, sizing, or runtime setting changed.
- **Executable capital replay:** The new 6h `+20%/-30%` models were then threshold-tuned on validation and replayed with `1.0` BNB starting equity, `0.1` BNB fixed stakes, and an 8-position cap. The classifier produced `+0.1380` BNB on validation and `-0.4649` BNB on final; the profit ranker produced `+0.3981` BNB on validation and `-0.1000` BNB on final. Both are rejected; no model or runtime setting changed.
- **Conclusion:** Longer labels and a profit-ranking objective are worth continuing, but this window does not provide a deployable standard. Require a new independent window, complete-path support, conservative censor handling, and positive validation/final cost stress before considering a live switch. `docs/model_scoreboard.md` was updated for this round.

## 2026-09-13 Fresh Window Short-vs-Long Comparison

- **Rejected / research only:** A new normalized window ending `2026-09-13T13:50:36Z` contains `232,549` deduplicated tokens and `3,011` entry candidates. Nested buy/sell and price paths were sorted by chain timestamp, stale `last_update` values were repaired, and the integrity scan found no malformed rows, future or pre-creation events, duplicate output tokens, or ordering violations.
- **Fresh models:** Regularized CatBoost barrier/ranker models were retrained from this window for `1h`, `6h`, and `24h` horizons with purged chronological splits, then replayed at `1.0` BNB equity, `0.1` BNB stakes, and an 8-position cap. The final split stayed sealed for model and threshold selection.
- **Short horizon:** A fixed top-1% `1h/+20%/-30%` barrier ranker was positive in all four expanding walk-forward slices under base costs (`+0.1114` BNB cumulative), while the uncapped `1h` ranker reached `+0.0830` BNB. These are small-sample research diagnostics; moderate-cost cumulative profit was only `+0.0068` BNB for the barrier model and `-0.0502` BNB for uncapped, with harsh-cost cumulative profit `-0.1156` and `-0.1564` BNB.
- **Long horizon:** The `6h` and `24h` candidates did not retain positive final profit. The `24h` uncapped `+100%` target had too few positive training labels to fit; lowering the learnability threshold still produced negative validation and final cash replays. Complete-path means also deteriorated as horizon increased, while censoring increased sharply.
- **Market regime:** The fetched BSC DEX volume series peaked near `$2.3B` on September 8-9 and fell to about `$0.79B/$0.71B` on September 12-13. The regime shift matches the final-split degradation and argues for short, flow-sensitive entries with a time-based risk exit rather than multi-day blind holding.
- **Conclusion:** Keep the current runtime model and trading settings unchanged. The short `1h` direction is the next shadow candidate, but it is not live-ready until a fresh independent window passes moderate and harsh execution stress with a predeclared trade count. `docs/model_scoreboard.md` was updated for this round.

Selected trade-delta showed final added overlay trades had `233` trades, `0.33476394849785407` win rate, and `-3054.008380750268%` summed return, while removed baseline trades had `20` trades, `0.6` win rate, and `447.8777875379374%` summed return. The strict legacy support candidates `12-15` added only `7` overlay entries but still failed validation profit and stress gates. - 2026-05-27 post-target continue-hold activation replay: accepted as a strict offline replay-only candidate for the current v95 live stack; not live-switched. SmartSearch evidence under `docs/research/20260527-post-target-continue-hold-overlay/` supported path-dependent triple-barrier/meta-label and survival/time-to-event framing. Trade-delta attribution had no worsened common trades (`validation 2 improved/0 worsened`, `final 4 improved/0 worsened`). Decision: accepted offline replay candidate, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, bot restart, or live switch change; next node is live-runtime integration plus live-switch-level review if deploying.

- 2026-05-28 default-off live-runtime integration: implemented the reviewed runtime availability layer for the accepted `continue_hold` overlay, but did not enable it. - 2026-05-27 action-router positive pass-through replay: converted the router's strict replay experiment into a no-entry-harm mode where `skip`, missing route, low confidence, and unsupported route decisions pass through to baseline behavior, while only high-confidence positive routes can modify exits. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-action-policy-router-positive-passthrough/summary.md` and `data/replay_reports/action_policy_router_replay_20260527_positive_passthrough.json`. The mode removed the entry-side damage: validation and final primary metrics tied baseline (`0.021094872146` BNB validation, `0.005174515325` BNB final). It still rejected: validation/final `action_policy_router_quick_take_profit_entry_count=0`, so the route model did not produce positive overlay activity on actual accepted entries, and stress worst profit stayed lower (`0.011148541483 -> 0.009316506809` validation, `0.001874747768 -> 0.001844250439` final). Decision: no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Next direction should train/diagnose on actual baseline entry indices or trade logs, then test a post-entry exit overlay with labels aligned to trades that strict replay actually opens.
- 2026-05-27 action-router flow-alias replay: fixed the previous strict replay feature-parity collapse by aliasing extractor fields into the router's `flow_*` decision-time names and forcing `include_flow_features=true` only for the offline router replay script. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-action-policy-router-flow-alias/summary.md` and `data/replay_reports/action_policy_router_replay_20260527_flow_alias.json`. Feature parity recovered: route model features now include `flow_buy_sell_ratio_30s`, `flow_total_volume_60s`, `flow_total_volume_10s`, `flow_signed_imbalance_30s`, and other flow fields. Result still rejected: validation net profit fell (`0.021094872146 -> 0.018628768892` BNB), final net profit fell (`0.005174515325 -> 0.004750771325` BNB), final win rate fell (`52.38% -> 45.00%`), and final max drawdown worsened. Diagnosis: the hard `skip` route rejected profitable baseline entries, while strict replay had `0` quick-profit overlay entries, so there was no compensating positive route. Decision: no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Next direction should be a positive-only / pass-through router where `skip` preserves baseline behavior and only high-confidence positive routes may change exit handling.
- 2026-05-27 multi-policy action-router replay: SmartSearch-backed selective-classification / learning-to-defer / MoE-routing research tested a structurally different route model instead of another binary support gate. Code added `src/pipeline/action_policy_router_probe.py`, `scripts/probe_action_policy_router.py`, replay-only `action_policy_routes_by_episode` support, and `scripts/run_action_policy_router_replay.py`. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-action-policy-router-multiroute/summary.md`, shadow probe `data/replay_reports/action_policy_router_probe_20260527_multiroute_best.json`, and strict replay `data/replay_reports/action_policy_router_replay_20260527_multiroute.json`. Shadow routing looked positive (`validation +2574.4838%`, final/latest `+1157.8294%` selected reward), but strict replay rejected it: validation net profit fell (`0.021094872146 -> 0.020911793965` BNB), final net profit fell (`0.005174515325 -> 0.005083918933` BNB), final win rate fell (`52.38% -> 50.00%`), and stress profit/return were lower. Diagnosis: the strict replay route map had feature parity collapse; only `flow_metrics_available`, `near_threshold_rescue_used`, `pred_return`, and `prob` were common, so the learned router lost the flow features that drove the shadow result. Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Next direction should fix replay candidate-row decision-time flow feature parity before retesting a router.
- 2026-05-27 delayed-confirmation quick-profit replay: SmartSearch-backed false-breakout / order-flow-confirmation research tested whether waiting a few seconds before quick-profit overlay entry can filter first-tick fakeouts without reopening the previously rejected broad quick-TP branch. Code added replay-only quick-profit confirmation delay and price-hold/chase params (`buy_quick_profit_overlay_confirmation_delay_seconds`, `buy_quick_profit_overlay_max_confirmation_drawdown_pct`, `buy_quick_profit_overlay_max_confirmation_chase_pct`) plus `scripts/run_delayed_confirmation_quick_tp_replay.py`. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-delayed-confirmation-quick-profit/summary.md`, live TTB report `data/replay_reports/time_to_barrier_probe_20260527_live_rejects_since_midnight.json`, and strict replay `data/replay_reports/delayed_confirmation_quick_tp_replay_20260527.json`. Best validation candidate used `5s` confirmation delay, `3%` max drawdown, `20%` max chase, and `35%` quick TP; it narrowly improved validation net profit (`0.02109487 -> 0.02113251` BNB) with only `5` overlay entries, but worsened validation max drawdown, WF worst return, and stress profit. Final confirmation failed: net profit fell (`0.00517452 -> 0.00462917` BNB), trades rose (`21 -> 23`), win rate weakened (`0.5238 -> 0.5217`), and WF/stress gates failed. Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Do not continue static short-delay quick-profit tuning as the next main branch; richer decision-time collapse/toxicity separation or replacement-oracle support is required.
- 2026-05-27 same-token replacement support-window replay: SmartSearch-backed dynamic-treatment / delayed-offline-RL / OPE review tested whether the prior same-token early-replacement label only failed because the `20s` window was too tight. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-replacement-support-window/summary.md` and strict replay `data/replay_reports/runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay_20260527_early_replacement60.json`. The replay made no code or runtime changes; it preserved base candidates and widened only `--early-replacement-max-lead-seconds` from `20` to `60`. Result: reject. Raw train support stayed exactly `15/619787` positives, feature importance again collapsed to `pred_return=1.0`, validation candidate tied baseline (`32` trades, `0.021094872146` BNB, `75.00%` win rate), and final candidate tied baseline (`21` trades, `0.005174515325` BNB, `52.38%` win rate). Trade delta was empty on validation and final (`0` added trades, `0` removed trades, all common trades unchanged). Decision: no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Do not continue mechanically widening this exact replacement label to `120s`/`300s`; the next useful branch is a replacement-only oracle upper-bound diagnostic or a richer early-collapse/toxicity separator.
- 2026-05-27 replacement-only oracle upper-bound diagnostic: SmartSearch-backed CPE/OPE and triple-barrier review led to a reusable ex-post diagnostic, not a deployable estimator. Evidence: `docs/research/20260527-replacement-oracle-upper-bound/summary.md`, phase-1 report `data/replay_reports/replacement_oracle_upper_bound_20260527_phase1.json`, and barrier report `data/replay_reports/replacement_oracle_upper_bound_20260527_barrier.json`. Phase 1 showed the direction is not support-dead: validation had `251/290/341/369` replacement pairs for `20/60/120/300s`, final had `154/171/212/254`, and pre-baseline move p75 was roughly `26%-40%`. Phase 2 rejected blanket replacement under `560s`, `+25%` take-profit and `-18%` stop barriers: validation and final `delta_realized_pct_p50` were `0.0` for every window, so the report rejected with `delta_realized_p50_below_min`. MFE deltas and profit-first ratios are distributionally positive, but they do not justify a live model, threshold, sizing, `.env`, model artifact, or bot restart change. Next work, if staying on this branch, must be a decision-time selector over replacement pairs and must beat strict replay gates; do not deploy or tune blanket early replacement.

The best balanced strict grid on the current fresh validation slice (`max_depth=3`, `min_samples_leaf=50`, `probability_threshold=0.20`) selected `25/50` candidates with `19/25` precision, split `14` accepted and `11` rejected; the rejected selections concentrated in `fast_profit`, `fast_profit_then_collapse`, `flat_timeout`, and `stop_first`. - 2026-05-26 runner-retention label probe: SmartSearch-backed triple-barrier / landmark / competing-risk review plus a new read-only probe (`src/pipeline/runner_retention_label_probe.py`, `scripts/probe_runner_retention_label_support.py`) showed that a replay-equivalent slow-runner label is feasible for shadow replay but still not live-switch evidence. The positive rule is `+25%` after `180s` and `+60%` before a `-18%/-25%` stop inside the `600s` horizon. On a `9-file` no-cache fast slice with `shadow_min_prob=0.85`, sample positives were `151/3/31` but unique-token positives were only `72/2/21`, so validation failed the token support gate. Decision: no live switch, no `.env` change, and no model artifact change. Use this label only for shadow replay / future replay-integrated candidate gates until more same-shape live evidence accumulates.

Evidence: `docs/research/20260526-conditional-exit-flow-survival/summary.md`, `data/replay_reports/live_trade_attribution_20260526_conditional_exit_flow_survival_round.json`, `data/replay_reports/time_to_barrier_probe_20260526_conditional_exit_flow_survival_round.json`, `data/replay_reports/support_action_policy_probe_20260526_conditional_exit_flow_survival_round.json`, and `data/replay_reports/competing_risk_quick_profit_replay_20260526_conditional_exit_flow_survival.json`. - 2026-05-26 dead-flow / accepted-entry loss attribution round: live attribution since `2026-05-26T00:00:00` still says `NO_GO_FOR_LIVE_SWITCH` (`4` closed trades, `0` wins, net `-0.0000978376` BNB; failure labels `dead_flow_timeout=3`, `unprofitable_other=1`). Reports: `data/replay_reports/flow_toxicity_meta_gate_replay_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round_probe.json` (`reject`, 60s flow gate never fired), `data/replay_reports/dead_bounce_veto_replay_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round.json` (`reject`, veto never fired), `data/replay_reports/dead_flow_exit_replay_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round.json` (`reject`, tiny validation uplift but final/stress weaker), `data/replay_reports/buyer_depth_contrast_replay_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round.json` (`reject`, static buyer/volume floors cut too much profit), `data/replay_reports/accepted_entry_feature_contrast_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round.json`, and `data/replay_reports/accepted_entry_loss_meta_gate_replay_20260526_dead_flow_toxicity_meta_gate_round.json`. The learned accepted-entry loss gate trained on `201` train trades (`keep=142`, `skip=59`) tested `9` keep-score floors from `0.05` through `0.65`; lenient floors produced no effective rejects, while the best mid-floor validation point at `buy_path_state_meta_gate_min_score=0.55` had real signal (`net_profit_bnb` `0.02109487 -> 0.02156772`, win rate `75.0% -> 80.0%`, stress worst profit `0.01114854 -> 0.01136128`). The live switch gate is not too strict; the tested static and learned accepted-entry toxicity gates are not robust enough. Keep the reusable attribution tools, but the next model direction should add stronger support/shift controls or richer temporal/path labels rather than deploying these gates.

Decision: reject for live use; keep the stable-LCB tooling as shadow-only evidence. Scoreboard updated and this experiment must not be mistaken for a live-switch candidate.
- 2026-05-27 runner-retention trade-delta diagnostic: reused the committed runner-retention / conditional slow-hold research from `docs/research/20260526-time-to-event-exit-dead-flow/summary.md` with a new replay trade-delta attribution angle. New reusable tooling in `src/pipeline/replay_trade_delta_attribution.py` and `scripts/run_runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay.py --write-selected-trade-delta` writes added/removed/common trade attribution from baseline vs candidate trade logs. Report: `data/replay_reports/runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay_20260527_trade_delta.json`; summary: `docs/research/20260527-runner-retention-trade-delta/summary.md`. The selected candidate again improved validation profit (`0.02109487 -> 0.02301520` BNB) but failed strict gates with trade expansion (`32 -> 42`), weaker validation win rate (`0.75 -> 0.6905`), worse max drawdown (`-9.8821% -> -18.6685%`), and worse stress drawdown. Final net profit improved slightly (`0.00517452 -> 0.00566445` BNB), but win rate weakened (`0.5238 -> 0.4783`) and final confirmation still failed. Trade-delta attribution showed validation added trades were mixed (`15` added, `8` wins, `7` losses), while final added trades were mostly toxic (`7` added, `1` win, `6` losses); common trades were unchanged, so the failure is at the added/removed boundary. Decision: no live switch; keep trade-delta attribution as material shadow-only tooling and use it to build a narrower runner-retention filter instead of widening thresholds.
- 2026-05-27 added-trade boundary policy probe: SmartSearch evidence under `docs/research/20260527-added-trade-boundary-policy-selection/summary.md` reframed the runner-retention failure as support-constrained, cost-sensitive added-action selection rather than another threshold sweep. Reusable attribution now writes matched decision-time feature rows, and the read-only probe `scripts/probe_added_trade_boundary_policy.py` selected a one-feature keep rule on validation added trades only, then evaluated it once on final. Evidence: `data/replay_reports/runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay_20260527_added_boundary_input.json` and `data/replay_reports/added_trade_boundary_policy_probe_20260527_runner_retention.json`. The selected validation rule was `retail_entry_rate_ratio_30s <= 1.1911726598514814`, keeping `11/15` validation added trades, preserving all `8` winners, cutting losses from `7` to `3`, and improving cost-adjusted utility by `+309.8078`. It did not generalize: on final it kept all `7/7` added trades, so final added losses stayed `6`, win rate stayed `1/7`, and final cost-adjusted utility delta was `0.0`. Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Do not promote single-feature added-boundary rules from validation-only utility unless they also reduce final added-trade loss exposure.
- 2026-05-27 preserve-base candidate rerun confirmation: reran the runner-retention candidate replay with `--preserve-base-candidates --write-selected-trade-delta` after the added-boundary rejection to verify whether protecting base candidates changed the strict conclusion. Evidence: `data/replay_reports/runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay_20260527_preserve_base_candidates.json` and `docs/research/20260527-preserve-base-candidate-rerun/summary.md`. The selected candidate matched the prior profit-improving but precision-weaker shape: validation net profit rose `0.02109487 -> 0.02301520` BNB, but win rate fell `75.00% -> 69.05%` and max drawdown worsened `-9.8821% -> -18.6685%`; final net profit rose `0.00517452 -> 0.00566445` BNB, but final win rate fell `52.38% -> 47.83%`, so final confirmation failed the win-rate gate. Final trade-delta attribution again showed toxic added entries (`7` added, `1` win, `6` losses). Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Commit this failed experiment separately and do not mix it with the next direction.
- 2026-05-27 same-token early-replacement runner-retention replay: SmartSearch-backed landmarking / selective-risk / OPE review under `docs/research/20260527-same-token-early-replacement/summary.md` reframed runner-retention rescue as a time-to-baseline-entry problem: learn only candidates that are slow-runner positives and become same-token baseline entries within `20s`. Code added `target_mode=runner_retention_early_replacement` in `src/pipeline/runner_retention_replay_gate.py` plus CLI support in `scripts/run_runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay.py`. Evidence: `data/replay_reports/runner_retention_candidate_gate_replay_20260527_early_replacement20.json`. The target was too sparse (`15` positives out of `619787` raw training candidates), feature importance collapsed to `pred_return`, and the strict replay became a no-op: validation and final selected candidates exactly matched baseline (`0` added trades, `0` removed trades). Final confirmation failed net-profit improvement because the candidate tied baseline instead of improving it. Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Do not use the `20s` early-replacement label as-is; next attempt should widen/reshape the replacement label or first quantify a replacement-only oracle upper bound.
- 2026-05-27 near-threshold hardening replay: after the stability-LCB / trade-delta failures, a strict replay tested whether simply disabling or hardening v95's near-threshold rescue pocket improves robustness. New CLI and tests: `scripts/run_near_threshold_hardening_replay.py`, `tests/model/test_near_threshold_hardening_replay_cli.py`; report `data/replay_reports/near_threshold_hardening_replay_20260526_stability_lcb_round.json`; summary `docs/research/20260526-near-threshold-hardening-replay/summary.md`. The selected validation candidate disabled near-threshold rescue entries, reducing near-threshold entries `3 -> 0`, but worsened validation net profit (`0.02109487 -> 0.01931958` BNB), win rate (`0.75 -> 0.7419`), max drawdown (`-9.8821% -> -13.0767%`), walk-forward worst drawdown (`-17.4320% -> -22.8939%`), final profit (`0.00517452 -> 0.00508392` BNB), and final win rate (`0.5238 -> 0.5`). Decision: reject, no live switch, no `.env`, threshold, sizing, model artifact, or bot restart change. Do not globally disable or harden the v95 near-threshold rescue; the next improvement must separate added-trade quality rather than deleting the whole rescue pocket.
